- **omics-hpc-skill**: 端到端管理 omics-hpc 集群——既覆盖腾讯云组学平台云 API（DescribeHPCClusters / RunCommand / DescribeCommandExecution），也内置 SLURM 作业（sbatch / squeue / sacct / scancel / scontrol / salloc / srun）与 tophpc 基础设施（cluster / node / queue / scale / fs / image / log / version）的命令生成与远程下发能力。覆盖以下场景：列出/筛选 HPC 集群、远程下发 Shell 命令、轮询 InvocationId 取回执行结果；提交/查询/取消 SLURM 作业、生成 sbatch 脚本、诊断 PENDING 原因；增删队列/节点、配置弹性伸缩、挂载文件系统、打镜像、查看 / 修改集群配置。触发关键词：HPC 集群、omics-hpc、DescribeHPCClusters、RunCommand、InvocationId、远程执行；sbatch、squeue、sacct、scancel、scontrol、salloc、srun、提交作业、查任务、取消任务、为什么任务没跑、生成脚本、依赖提交、申请交互节点；tophpc、加节点、删节点、加队列、删队列、弹性伸缩、扩缩容、挂载、卸载存储、打… ([infometa/workbuddyskills](https://github.com/infometa/workbuddyskills/tree/HEAD/experts/omics-hpc-expert/skills/omics-hpc-skill) · ★455)

- **omics-integration**: Multi-omics data integration using MOFA+, DIABLO, and MixOmics for combining genomics, transcriptomics, proteomics, metabolomics, and epigenomics datasets. Use for identifying shared variation across omics layers, building integrative biomarker models, and discovering cross-modal biological patterns. Best for paired multi-omics cohort studies. ([Lord1Egypt/scientific-agent-toolkit](https://github.com/Lord1Egypt/scientific-agent-toolkit/tree/HEAD/scientific-skills/omics-integration) · ★3 · 8 copies)
- **omics-metadata-json-extraction**: Use when you need to tabulate and verify paired-omics link metadata from Zenodo-archived PoDP JSON project files, e.g., reproducing registered genome–metabolome and BGC–MS/MS link structures and counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/omics-metadata-json-extraction))
- **omics-metadata-keyword-search**: Use when a research question asks whether keyword search over PoDP project metadata (e.g. extraction methods like 'methanol + cells') can retrieve the projects whose archived JSON documents match that extraction method. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/omics-metadata-keyword-search))
- **omics-missingness-simulation**: Use when a LUCID analysis involves sporadically missing omics entries where complete-case analysis is infeasible, and you need evidence that integrated EM-based imputation outperforms mean imputation before trusting downstream cluster estimates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/omics-missingness-simulation))
- **omics-ml-classifier**: Train and evaluate machine learning classifiers on omics data (gene expression, mutation scores, copy number, or any numeric feature matrix). Supports Random Forest, Logistic Regression, SVM, and XGBoost. Outputs cross-validation metrics, test set performance, feature importances, ROC curve, confusion matrix, and optional UMAP embedding. Works with any two-class or multi-class label. ([MDhewei/bioinfor-claw](https://github.com/MDhewei/bioinfor-claw/tree/HEAD/machine-learning-and-deep-learning/omics-ml-classifier) · ★65)
- **omics-network-construction-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating or comparing the computational efficiency of network construction tools (e.g. correlation-based co-occurrence builders) on synthetic or real omics datasets spanning a range of feature counts, to quantify how runtime and memory scale with dimensionality. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/omics-network-construction-benchmarking))
- **omics-network-feature-extraction**: Use when after you have built a network object (adjacency matrix, edge list, or correlation output) from omics data and need to quantitatively describe network properties beyond visualization. - **omics-network-plot-generation**: Use when the input is a correlation matrix deposited as a tabular csv/txt/xls/xlsx file (e.g. corr_net.csv) and the goal is a publication-ready network visualization of pairwise relationships between omics features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/omics-network-plot-generation))
- **omics-network-propagation**: Use when you have a set of significant omic hits (e.g., phosphoproteomic seed nodes from 105 breast cancer tumors) and a reference interactome, and need sample-specific networks plus functional modules for stratification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/omics-network-propagation))
- **omics-network-visualization-preparation**: Use when after constructing a correlation-based network from omics data (nodes and edges defined), when you need to assign visual positions to nodes for rendering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/omics-network-visualization-preparation))
- **omics-pilot-data-preparation**: Use when running MultiPower or MultiML sample-size/power estimation, since both algorithms require properly preprocessed pilot datasets per omic platform before the estimation procedure can run. - **omics-pipeline**: Run omics pipeline stages from reads QC and mapping to assembly, binning, gene calling, phylogenomics and structure, and open the annotation, viromics and HGT stage skills. - **omics-plotting**: omics-plotting: publication-style figure authoring for omics / bioinformatics results with matplotlib / seaborn. ([omicverse/omicverse-skills](https://github.com/omicverse/omicverse-skills/tree/HEAD/src/omicverse_skills/skills/bulk-celltype-deconvolution) · ★25)
- **omicverse-bulk-metabol-multivariate**: Multivariate discrimination and biomarker selection on a preprocessed metabolomics AnnData. Use when running PLS-DA, OPLS-DA, VIP / S-plot inspection, per-metabolite ROC AUC with bootstrap confidence intervals, or building a multi-metabolite biomarker panel with nested CV and a permutation null. Assumes the input has already been imputed, PQN-normalized, and log-then-Pareto-transformed. ([omicverse/omicverse-skills](https://github.com/omicverse/omicverse-skills/tree/HEAD/src/omicverse_skills/skills/bulk-metabol-multivariate) · ★25)

Use when converting `t_metabol_01_intro` or `t_metabol_06_batch_correction` into a reusable skill, when a user has a MetaboAnalyst CSV / LC-MS peak table to clean before differential or multivariate analysis, or when choosing among PQN/TIC/median sample-normalization, log/Pareto feature-transformation, and qrilc/knn/half_min/zero imputation strategies. Use when converting `t_metabol_04_untargeted` or `t_metabol_05_lipidomics` into a reusable skill, when the input feature IDs encode `m/z`/`RT`, or when the var_names look like `PC 34:1` / `Cer d18:1/24:0` / `TAG 54:3`. ([omicverse/omicverse-skills](https://github.com/omicverse/omicverse-skills/tree/HEAD/src/omicverse_skills/skills/bulk-metabol-untargeted-lipidomics) · ★25)
- **omicverse-cross-modal-celltype-transfer**: Transfer cell-type labels from a reference AnnData to a query AnnData with OmicVerse weighted KNN over a shared embedding. Use when converting OmicVerse cross-modal annotation notebooks into a reusable skill, when labeling an ATAC query from an RNA reference, or when you need the weighted_knn_trainer / weighted_knn_transfer workflow plus optional visualization. ([omicverse/omicverse-skills](https://github.com/omicverse/omicverse-skills/tree/HEAD/src/omicverse_skills/skills/cross-modal-celltype-transfer) · ★25)
- **omicverse-micro-metabol-paired**: Paired microbiome × metabolomics integration on sample-aligned AnnDatas. Use when you have paired microbe-counts + metabolite-intensity tables on the same samples and want to find microbe-metabolite pairs that covary, or build a shared latent space. ([omicverse/omicverse-skills](https://github.com/omicverse/omicverse-skills/tree/HEAD/src/omicverse_skills/skills/micro-metabol-paired) · ★25)
- **omicverse-microbiome-16s-amplicon-dada2**: 16S rRNA amplicon analysis from raw FASTQs to a samples × ASVs AnnData with 7-rank SINTAX taxonomy, plus the canonical alpha / beta / ordination / DA stack. Use when running the vsearch-or-DADA2 amplicon pipeline (`ov.alignment.amplicon_16s_pipeline`), when ingesting an existing OTU/ASV count table with `build_amplicon_anndata`, or when computing Shannon / Bray-Curtis / PCoA / Wilcoxon-DA on the resulting AnnData. ([omicverse/omicverse-skills](https://github.com/omicverse/omicverse-skills/tree/HEAD/src/omicverse_skills/skills/microbiome-16s-amplicon-dada2) · ★25)
- **omicverse-microbiome-da-comparison**: Run all three differential-abundance methods (Wilcoxon, pyDESeq2, ANCOM-BC) on the same microbiome AnnData, compare their hit sets via 3-way Venn / overlap counts, and decide which to trust on a given cohort. Use when the user wants to benchmark DA methods on a 16S study, when picking between methods on a small or zero-inflated cohort, or when reporting consensus features that survive multiple tests. ([omicverse/omicverse-skills](https://github.com/omicverse/omicverse-skills/tree/HEAD/src/omicverse_skills/skills/microbiome-da-comparison) · ★25)
- **omicverse-microbiome-meta-analysis**: Combine multiple per-study microbiome AnnDatas into a single cross-cohort table and run inverse-variance / random-effects meta-analysis on differential abundance. Use when you have 16S studies from multiple cohorts that need joint analysis, when you need a combined log2 fold-change with Cochran's I² heterogeneity, or when you want to find features whose effect replicates across cohorts (vs. cohort-specific signals). ([omicverse/omicverse-skills](https://github.com/omicverse/omicverse-skills/tree/HEAD/src/omicverse_skills/skills/microbiome-meta-analysis) · ★25)
- **omicverse-microbiome-phylogeny**: Build an ASV-level phylogenetic tree (MAFFT alignment + FastTree GTR+Γ), attach it to a 16S AnnData, and run phylogenetically-aware diversity (Faith PD, weighted / unweighted UniFrac). Use when adding tree-aware metrics on top of the basic 16S amplicon AnnData, or when the cohort needs UniFrac-driven beta-diversity instead of Bray-Curtis. ([omicverse/omicverse-skills](https://github.com/omicverse/omicverse-skills/tree/HEAD/src/omicverse_skills/skills/microbiome-phylogeny) · ★25)
- **omicverse-reference-label-transfer**: Transfer cell labels from a reference AnnData to a query AnnData with OmicVerse AnnotationRef. Use when converting OmicVerse reference-annotation notebooks into a reusable, triggerable skill, choosing a label-transfer backend such as harmony, scVI, or scanorama, or preparing paired query/reference single-cell data for weighted kNN annotation. ([omicverse/omicverse-skills](https://github.com/omicverse/omicverse-skills/tree/HEAD/src/omicverse_skills/skills/reference-label-transfer) · ★25)
- **omicverse-single-cell-annotation**: Annotate single-cell AnnData with OmicVerse using the CellTypist, gpt4celltype, or SCSA branches. Use when turning OmicVerse annotation notebooks into a reusable, triggerable skill, selecting a backend, or mapping a clustered AnnData object to cell-type labels. ([omicverse/omicverse-skills](https://github.com/omicverse/omicverse-skills/tree/HEAD/src/omicverse_skills/skills/single-cell-annotation) · ★25)
- **omicverse-single-cell-batch-integration**: Run OmicVerse single-cell batch integration as a reusable, triggerable skill after preprocessing is already complete. ([omicverse/omicverse-skills](https://github.com/omicverse/omicverse-skills/tree/HEAD/src/omicverse_skills/skills/single-cell-batch-integration) · ★25)
- **omicverse-single-cell-cellmatch-ontology**: Map free-text cell-type annotations to the Cell Ontology (CL) via NLP-based sentence-transformer matching, with optional LLM-driven abbreviation expansion and Cell Taxonomy taxonomy enrichment. Use when standardising author-shorthand cell-type names to canonical CL terms (e.g. 'TIL-1' → 'tissue-resident memory CD8+ T cell, CL:0000625'), when running cross-cohort label harmonisation, or when reproducing `t_cellmatch`. ([omicverse/omicverse-skills](https://github.com/omicverse/omicverse-skills/tree/HEAD/src/omicverse_skills/skills/single-cell-cellmatch-ontology) · ★25)
- **omicverse-single-cell-cellphonedb-communication**: Analyze single-cell cell-cell communication with OmicVerse CellPhoneDB and CellChat-style visualization. Use when converting an OmicVerse CellPhoneDB notebook into a reusable skill, when running CellPhoneDB ligand-receptor analysis on annotated AnnData, or when choosing pathway aggregation, layout, signaling-role, and bubble-plot branches for downstream communication summaries. ([omicverse/omicverse-skills](https://github.com/omicverse/omicverse-skills/tree/HEAD/src/omicverse_skills/skills/single-cell-cellphonedb-communication) · ★25)
- **omicverse-single-cell-cellrank-fate**: CellRank fate maps from RNA velocity. Use after RNA velocity is computed (scvelo / dynamo / latentvelo / graphvelo) and before reporting fate probabilities or marker dynamics. ([omicverse/omicverse-skills](https://github.com/omicverse/omicverse-skills/tree/HEAD/src/omicverse_skills/skills/single-cell-cellrank-fate) · ★25)
- **omicverse-single-cell-cellvote-consensus**: Multi-annotator consensus for single-cell labels via `ov.single.CellVote`. ([omicverse/omicverse-skills](https://github.com/omicverse/omicverse-skills/tree/HEAD/src/omicverse_skills/skills/single-cell-cellvote-consensus) · ★25)
